<script type="text/javascript">
        //homescript.init();
        homeIndexOnlyPage.run();
        var player = "";
        var playermain = "";
        function onPlayerReady(event) {
        }

        function onPlayerStateChange(event) {
            if (event.data == YT.PlayerState.ENDED) {
                $('.website-feature-iframe').addClass("hidden-iframe");
                $('.feature-image-advertising-video-home').removeClass("hidden");
                $('.feature-image-advertising-video-home').fadeIn();
            }
        }

        function onPlayerStateChangeMain(event) {
            if (event.data == YT.PlayerState.ENDED) {

                $('.website-home-iframe-1').addClass("hidden-iframe");
                $('.main-image-advertising-video-home').removeClass("hidden");
                $('.main-image-advertising-video-home').fadeIn();
            }
        }

        function onYouTubePlayerAPIReady(event) {
            playerMain = new YT.Player('youtubeMain', {
                events: {
                    'onReady': onPlayerReady,
                    'onStateChange': onPlayerStateChangeMain
                }
            });
            player = new YT.Player('youtube', {
                events: {
                    'onReady': onPlayerReady,
                    'onStateChange': onPlayerStateChange
                }
            });
        }

        $(document).ready(function() {
            $("#btn-ft-email-subscribe").on('click', function() {
                var value = $("#input-ft-email-subscribe").val();
                var spinner = new Spinner().spin();
                commonJs.loading.start(spinner);
                if (value.trim() == "") {
                    commonJs.loading.end(spinner);
                    commonJs.alert({ text: "This field is required!", type: "warning" });
                } else {
                    if (!commonJs.validateEmail(value)) {
                        commonJs.loading.end(spinner);
                        commonJs.alert({ text: "Email is invalid", type: "warning" });
                    } else {
                        $.ajax({
                            url: "/MasterPage/VerifyEmail",
                            type: "POST",
                            data: {
                                email: value
                            },
                            dataType: 'json',
                            success: function(result) {
                                commonJs.loading.end(spinner);
                                if (result == 1) {
                                    $.ajax({
                                        url: "/MasterPage/SaveEmailSubcribe",
                                        type: "POST",
                                        data: {
                                            data:value,
                                            socialNewworkTypeID: 0,
                                            subTypeID : 1

                                        },
                                        dataType: 'json',
                                        success: function(result2) {
											//if (result2.StatusCode == 1) {
											//    location.href = result2.Data;
											//} else {
											//    location.href = '/thank-you-for-subscribing';
													//}

													commonJs.alert({ text: "You are already signed up.", type: "warning" });
												} else {
													location.href = "/email-sign-up-thank-you";
												}
										}
                                    });
                                } else {
                                    commonJs.alert({ text: "Email is invalid", type: "warning" });
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    }
                }
            });
            $("#input-ft-email-subscribe").keyup(function(e) {

                }
            });
